# Paper claims

Every number printed in the arXiv paper: where it appears (tex file and line in the arXiv LaTeX source), what it measures, the script that produced it, the released file(s) it is recomputed from, the rule, the recomputed value and whether it matches. Generated by `python scripts/verify_release.py --write-docs` from `scripts/claims/part_*.py`; do not edit by hand. The same rows, machine-readable, are in [paper_numbers.json](paper_numbers.json).

Kinds: `measured` (recomputed from result rows), `count` (size of a data set), `config` (a hyperparameter or protocol constant, read from a config, `train_meta.json` or the experiment code), `derived` (arithmetic on other recomputed numbers), `log` (exists only as printed text: parsed from a shipped text table, or recorded from the private project log), `external` (quoted from a cited paper).

Rows: 1136. Recomputed and matched: 1111. Mismatched: 0. Recorded from the private project log, not recomputed: 5. External (cited papers): 20.

## Recorded, not recomputed

These numbers exist only in the private project log (GPU sizes, spend, session count, one pre-registered bar). Their logged values are stored here and in `paper_numbers.json` with `"source": "project log (private)"`; `verify_release.py` reports them and does not fail on them.

| e.app.compute.gpu_hi | 48 | sections/appendix.tex:186 | largest GPU memory used (GB) | 48 |
